BENGALURU, THURSDAY, 01, FEBRUARY, 2024(MAGHA, 12, SHAKAVARSHA, 1945) Volume - 159 Issue 24

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS0ADMN/122/2023-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1265441 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru-560 001, Date:23-01-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification . RD 368 PFH 2022 Dated 08-12-2022
regarding altering the limits of Dannur and Nellur village of Aland taluk and
Kalaburgi District and constituting a new revenue village called Dannur Tanda and
Gayamal Tanda was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD
368 PFH 2022 dated 08-12-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Kalaburgi district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 09-12-2022 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Dannur and Nellur of Aland taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new
revenue village called Dannur Tanda of Aland taluk Kalaburagidistrict along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

SCHEDULE-2
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Gayamala Tanda village of Aland Taluk
of Kalaburgi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary
of Dannur Revenue village and same will herein after be named as Gayama Tand of
Dannur village in Aland Taluk of Kalaburgi District.

Bengaluru, Date: 06-12-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Somalapura Village of
Turuvekere taluk Tumkur District and to constitute a new revenue villages
called Somalapura Gollarahatti and Venkatapura as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Somalapura village for conversion of Somalapura Gollarahatti and Venkatapura
unrecorded habitation in to hamlet/ extension/ part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Tumkur District.

SCHEDULE- 01
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of called Somalapura Gollarahatti village
of Turuvekere Taluk of Tumkur District convert the same to form
hamlet/extension within the boundary of Somalapura Revenue village and same
will herein after be named as called Somalapura Gollarahatti of Somalapura
village in Turuvekere Taluk of Tumkur District.

E-1218540
Bengaluru, Date: 06-12-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Agrahara Village of Madhugiri

taluk Tumkur District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Mulabagilapalya and sub village called Vaddarahatti as specified in schedule
along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Tumkur District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Agrahara of Madhugiri taluk Tumkur District to form a new revenue
village called as Mulabagilapalya of Madhugiri taluk Tumkur District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Nademavinapura Village of
Kunigal taluk Tumkur District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Kallegowdanapalya as specified in schedule.

Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Nademavinapura village for conversion of Kallegowdanapalya unrecorded
habitation in to hamlet/ extension/ part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Tumkur District.

SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of called Kallegowdanapalya village of
Kunigal Taluk of Tumkur District convert the same to form hamlet/extension
within the boundary of Nademavinapura Revenue village and same will herein
after be named as called Kallegowdanapalya of Nademavinapura village in Gubbi
Taluk of Tumkur District.

PR-235
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RC/22/2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building

E-1218540
Bengaluru, Date: 06-12-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land

Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Pura Village of Gubbi taluk
Tumkur District and to constitute a new revenue villages called Pura
Gollarahatti as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers
and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Tumkur District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Pura of Gubbi taluk Tumkur District to form a new revenue village called
as Pura Gollarahatti of Gubbi taluk Tumkur District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Yalacheehalli Village of Gubbi

taluk Tumkur District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Budensab palya as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Yalacheehalli village for conversion of Budensab palya unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/ extension/ part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Tumkur District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of called Budensab palya village of
Gubbi Taluk of Tumkur District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within
the boundary of Yalacheehalli Revenue village and same will herein after be named
as called Budensab palya of Yalacheehalli village in Gubbi Taluk of Tumkur
District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Devarabetta Village of
Pavagada taluk Tumkur District and to constitute a new revenue villages
called Maradipalya as specified in schedule.

Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Devarabetta village for conversion of Maradipalya unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/ extension/ part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or

after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Tumkur District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of called Maradipalya village of
Pavagada Taluk of Tumkur District convert the same to form hamlet/extension
within the boundary of Devarabetta Revenue village and same will herein after be
named as called Maradipalya of Devarabetta village in Pavagada Taluk of
Tumkur District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 23 KAMGRARA 2017 Dated:
21-01-2017 regarding altering the limits of Thamlapura village of Huvinahadagali
Taluk and Vijayanagara District and constituting a new revenue village called
Kagalakattithanda was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. 23
dated 09-02-2017 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Vijayanagara district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:09-10-2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Thamlapura Taluk in Huvinahadagali Vijayanagara District to form a new
revenue village called as Kagalakattithanda of Huvinahadagali Taluk Vijayanagara
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of ORVAI Village of KURUGODU
Taluk, BALLARI District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
MARUTHI CAMP as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey
numbers and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Ballari District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing Survey Numbers of The Villages and Survey Numbers of Theareas Specified
in Column (2) of The Table below Shall be Excluded from The limits of Said Villages of BALLARI
District KURUGODU Taluk to from a New Revenue Village called as MARUTHI CAMP Village of
KURUGODU Taluk, BALLARI District along with the Survey Numbers Included in Column (4)

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand

under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of KAPPAGALLU Village of
BALLARI Taluk, BALLARI District and to constitute a new revenue villages
called BALAJI NAGAR CAMP as specified in schedule along with maps with
old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Ballari District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village BALLARI District BALLARI Taluk to form a new revenue village called
BALAJI NAGAR CAMP Village of BALLARI Taluk, BALLARI District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 25 GLH 2022, Bengaluru. Dated
09-02-2022 regarding altering the limits of BHAIRADEVANAHALLI Village of
BALLARI Taluk and BALLARI District and constituting a new revenue village
SHIVAPURA was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No 155 Part 4A
Dated 14-02-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner BALLARI District.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 11-04-2022. And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received ;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of BHAIRADEVANAHALLI Village of BALLARI Taluk and BALLARI to
form a new revenue village called SHIVAPURA Village of BALLARI Taluk,
BALLARI District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 28,29 GLH 2023 Dated
13/02/2023 regarding altering the limits of Kalingeri Village of Sandur Taluk and
Ballari District and constituting a new revenue village called Dodda Upparahalli and
Hiral was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act,

1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette no Part no 4A page no
4436 to 4445 and 4454 to 4469 Gazette Dated 04/03/2023 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner Ballari District.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 11/10/2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received ;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Nagoor of Kalingeri Village of Sandur Taluk in Ballari District to form a new
revenue village called as Dodda Upparahalli of Sandur Taluk Ballari District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

Date:19-01-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 28,29 GLH 2023 Dated
13/02/2023 regarding altering the limits of Kalingeri Village of Sandur Taluk and
Ballari District and constituting a new revenue village called Dodda Upparahalli and
Hiral was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette no Part no 4A page no
4436 to 4445 and 4454 to 4469 Gazette Dated 04/03/2023 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from

the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner Ballari District.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 11/10/2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received ;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Nagoor of Kalingeri Village of Sandur Taluk in Ballari District to form a new
revenue village called as Dodda Upparahalli of Sandur Taluk Ballari District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No: RD 01 GLH 2023 Dated 09-01-2023
regarding altering the limits of of Chikke keryaginahalli Village of Sandur Taluk and
Bellari District and constituting a new revenue village called Sri Ramashiettyhalli
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette Dated 11/01/2023 Part no 4A
page no 2082 to 2097 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to
be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Kalaburagi District.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 11/10/2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received ;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Chikkereyaginahalli Village of SandurTaluk in Ballari District to form a new
revenue village called as Sri Ramashettihalli of SandurTaluk Ballari District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

NOTIFICATION
As per the No Objection received from the Ministry of Home Affairs
Government of India, vide letter no:11/31/2023-M&G, Dated:22.12.2023 and as per the
correct and approved spelling in Devanagri and Roman script by the Ministry of Science and
Technology, Government of India vide official Memorandum no SM/28/28/2023, DATED :
01.12.2023 the name of " Kittur Taluk " as "Chennammana Kitturu” Taluk of Belagavi
District, Karnataka State.

Language (Kannada) script of the new name will be as under:-
